# Broker ↔ exchange, clearing corporation and depository file register

| Register control | Value / evidence rule |
| --- | --- |
| Scope and cut-off | [verified] Prepared for the requested greenfield NSE+BSE+MCX broker scope, with public-source research cut-off **2026-10-15**. Cash, equity derivatives, currency derivatives, commodity derivatives, and connected depository operations. |
| Extent | [verified] **611 register rows**, including separately named variants, shared containers, historical crosswalks and explicit gaps. [unknown] Exhaustive production inventory across private member portals: this cannot be certified from public documents alone. |
| Evidence classes | **[verified]** = primary source supports the immediately following claim, with URL in the same row's evidence column; **[likely]** requires two independent named secondary sources; **[uncertain]** = weak evidence or explicitly identified analyst inference; **[unknown]** = not established. No [likely] assertion is needed here. |
| How to read a row | A class applies until the next class in that cell. The row's linked primary source supports its [verified] filename, issuer, route, direction, segment, timing or format claims. Different sources are named where a row combines schema and timing evidence. Business consumers and failure consequences are **[uncertain] analyst dependency assessments**, not exchange promises or automatic legal penalties. |
| Naming fidelity | [verified] Names are transcribed from published specifications or dated official download listings. Date-stamped examples are explicitly examples. Source placeholders, capitalization, suffixes, spaces and conflicting names are preserved. [unknown] A source typo's operational correction unless a second primary artifact establishes it. |
| Unverified names | **[unknown] filename** means the business interface is registered without manufacturing a basename. An extension, report code, portal screen, API method or business process is not automatically a file name. |
| Timing discipline | [verified] Where a source states only BOD, intraday, EOD, post-confirmation or post-settlement, the register retains that event. [unknown] An exact clock or arrival latency unless separately evidenced. A date token, `_0730`, `_1530`, `_hhmm`, snapshot timestamp, settlement deadline or member upload cutoff is not automatically publication time. |
| Member roles | [verified] Published clearing catalogues distinguish TM and CM variants; depository catalogues address DPs. [uncertain] A broker consumes a CM/DP-only file directly only if it has that role/access, otherwise through its appointed CM/DP integration. [unknown] This broker's actual entitlements and contracts. |
| Direction | Direction is from the broker/member's perspective. [verified] MCXCCL's manual sometimes names member-upload directories `DNLD` and response directories `UPLD`; these server-side labels do not reverse the arrows below. [MCXCCL manual](https://www.mcxccl.com/docs/mcxccllibraries/resources/mcxccl_file-formats-(version-1).pdf?sfvrsn=3bf284b9_1). |
| Schema status | [verified] A published historical format remains evidence of that format. [unknown] Its continued production use, later amendment or retirement unless expressly established. “Published”, “notified”, “effective”, “observed listing” and “proposal” are deliberately distinct. |
| Transport status | [unknown] No live authenticated member Extranet, ENIT, clearing portal, SFTP, Easiest or DPM account was used. Routes are only named when supported by the linked source. Public website endpoints are not represented as contracted APIs or availability guarantees. |

## Publication, migration and timing controls

| Control | Evidence / operational interpretation | Primary source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [verified] NSE/NCL exchange versus clearing reports | [verified] The exchange UDiFF convention and NCL clearing filenames are separate source identifiers; segment and member-role tokens must be retained. [uncertain] Reconcile exchange fills to the designated clearing corporation rather than assuming one file replaces the other. | [NCL26](https://www.nseclearing.in/sites/default/files/2026-05/Annexure%20B_1.xlsx) |
| [verified] NCL 2024→2026 UDiFF mappings | [verified] The 2024 mapping explicitly pairs native/first-standard files to UDiFF; the published 2026 catalogue adds settlement rounds and direct-payout reports. [unknown] Unlisted current member-specific supplements and amendments. | [CMPT60764](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/CMPT60764.zip); [NCL26](https://www.nseclearing.in/sites/default/files/2026-05/Annexure%20B_1.xlsx) |
| [verified] NCL cash final obligations | [verified] In the 2024 guidance, T+1 final obligations are conditional on changed CP/allocated institutional obligations; the T-day report is final for unaffected members. [uncertain] Absence of an F file is not by itself evidence of a failed feed. | [CMPT60764](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/CMPT60764.zip) |
| [verified] NSE DAT bhavcopy transition | [verified] MSD74764, dated 2026-06-18, notifies phased Extranet DAT bhavcopy availability from July 2026 and says segment dates will be communicated separately. It notifies discontinuation of existing MS/MD files and NNF bhavcopy broadcasts effective 2026-10-12. [unknown] Segment-specific DAT go-live notices and independently observed completion of the old-feed removal. | [MSD74764](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/MSD74764.pdf) |
| [verified] NSE exchange trade target times | [verified] MSD60556 is notified 2024-02-05, effective 2024-02-06; normal-session standard targets are CM 18:00, FO 19:30, CD 20:30 and commodity 01:00. [unknown] Current UDiFF-specific clock guarantee or special-session schedule. These are endeavour targets. | [MSD60556](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/MSD60556.pdf) |
| [verified] NCL clearing timing notices | [verified] The 2024-03-15 notices give named-file endeavour targets, not universal clearing deadlines. Member dashboard status is the stated means of tracking downloads. [unknown] Whether every target remains unchanged in October 2026. | [CMPT61172](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/CMPT61172.pdf); [CMPT61170](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/CMPT61170.pdf); [CD61173](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/CD61173.pdf) |
| [verified] MCX reference-data version | [verified] The PDF at a URL containing “2021” actually identifies v1.2 dated 2026-03-10 and immediate effect. The document, not the old URL text, establishes its version. [unknown] Actual member directory listing/spelling where older references differ. | [MCX-MASTER](https://www.mcxindia.com/docs/default-source/technology-services/new-trading-interfaces/mcxctcl5062021-mcx-reference-data-files-(masters).pdf?sfvrsn=d40d6956_2) |
| [verified] MCX UCC rollout | [verified] MCX circular index lists extension notices for the new UCC interface, including 2026-10-01. [unknown] Final effective activation of v1.1 as of this research. The old v1.0 PDF is historical evidence, not proof of the current upload contract. | [MCX-CIRC](https://www.mcxindia.com/circulars/all-circulars); [MCX-UCC](https://www.mcxindia.com/docs/default-source/technology-services/new-trading-interfaces/mcx-ucc-file-formats-(version-1-0).pdf?sfvrsn=e358c930_1) |
| [verified] CDSL proposal boundary | [verified] The harmonization page links v3.0.1.2 and explicitly labels v3.0.1.3 “Proposed format”. [unknown] Its effective implementation date. No proposed v3.0.1.3 field or filename is treated as an effective requirement here. | [CDSL-PAGE](https://www.cdslindia.com/DP/Harmonization.html); [CDSL-UD](https://www.cdslindia.com/downloads/DP/Harmonization/UDiFF%20Catalogue_V3.0.1.2.xlsx) |
| [verified] NSDL bundle boundary | [verified] NSDL publishes v3.0.1.2 and sample files in the linked bundle; the archive also contains v3.0.1.3. [unknown] Inclusion alone does not establish v3.0.1.3 go-live. This register extracts the v3.0.1.2 catalogue. | [NSDL-PAGE](https://nsdl.com/udiff); [NSDL-UD](https://nsdl.com/nsdl/2026-10/UDiFF%20catalouge%20V3.0.1.2%20and%20Updated%20Sample%20File-09102026.zip) |
| [verified] Depository EOD and split-file rules | [verified] v3.0.1.2 specifies EOD business date with zero time and sequence 999; incremental names carry system datetime. COD, SOH, SOT and client-master files may be split with a manifest and terminal `_p0.csv`. [unknown] Actual posting clock. | [CDSL-UD](https://www.cdslindia.com/downloads/DP/Harmonization/UDiFF%20Catalogue_V3.0.1.2.xlsx); [NSDL-UD](https://nsdl.com/nsdl/2026-10/UDiFF%20catalouge%20V3.0.1.2%20and%20Updated%20Sample%20File-09102026.zip) |
| [verified] Proposal versus consultation | [verified] The only proposal used as a status caution here is the explicitly proposed CDSL catalogue. [unknown] No consultation-paper proposal is promoted to an operational requirement; later notified implementation must be evidenced independently. | [CDSL-PAGE](https://www.cdslindia.com/DP/Harmonization.html) |

## Gaps and cautions

| Gap / caution | Unverified point or limit | Follow-up needed |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [unknown] Exhaustiveness | [unknown] No authenticated per-member directory inventory, file subscription list, version manifest or vendor back-office map was available. This is a researched register, not a certified list of every private operational file. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Obtain dated TM, CM and DP directory listings for each enabled segment; reconcile every actual file to a row. |
| [unknown] BSE current masters and bhavcopies | [unknown] A cash UDiFF CSV was downloaded and its exact example name verified. Current FO/CD/CO bhavcopy names, currency/spread masters, commodity masters and index constituent filenames remain unverified. Older BSE native schemas are labelled historical. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Retrieve current BSE member/common download manifests; do not synthesize BSE names from NSE UDiFF examples. |
| [unknown] NSE public download examples | [unknown] Dated listing evidence verifies the listed basename, not the decompressed internal file, field schema, completeness, every date substitution or contracted delivery SLA. DAT/LST/ZIP internal layouts remain unknown where not separately cited. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Fetch representative artifacts and current schema; verify header, checksum, trading date and decompression. |
| [unknown] Native / first-standard / UDiFF coexistence | [unknown] A historical native file may have been retired, supplemented or replaced. The register does not assert that every historical row should be scheduled. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Apply the relevant notified replacement/cutover mapping and confirm current member production samples. |
| [unknown] NCL catalogue inconsistencies | [unknown] 2026 catalogue repeats the cash TM trade row where a CM row might be expected; 2024 mapping separately verifies CM. Several Delivery names contain a space. `_P_0730` timing language differs between catalogues. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Preserve the source distinction; get live filenames and event semantics from NCL. Do not infer a 07:30 availability SLA. |
| [unknown] ICCL schema/sample inconsistencies | [unknown] Some sample trade identifiers differ from the segment schema; FO obligation index uses `0` while samples use `FOPHY`; Delivery whitespace and settlement-master samples differ. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Use the explicit schema for this register but obtain a current authoritative clarification before parser configuration. |
| [unknown] MCXCCL source conflicts | [unknown] Manual uses `Stamp_`; FAQ also prints `StampDuty_`. CTT status tokens differ. Embedded spaces, doubled prefixes and `CollateralOtward`/`Deallcoation` spellings occur in specifications. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Do not autocorrect or silently support guessed variants. Confirm exact production files and folders. |
| [unknown] MCX masters | [unknown] v1.2 spells `MCXScrips.bcp`; other older references can use a singular form. The March 2026 document verifies its printed spelling, not a live SFTP listing. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Confirm current reference-data directory and version; `.bcp` in this specification is CSV ASCII. |
| [unknown] SPAN and peak margin | [unknown] ICCL SPAN filenames, MCXCCL SPAN basenames/internal archive version, exact snapshot counts/times, and present ICCL peak-margin interface remain unverified. NCL price-snapshot times are not arrival times. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Obtain current risk-file manifests and compare parameter snapshot timestamps with receipt logs. |
| [unknown] Margin upload coverage | [unknown] NCL CM/FO batch schemas and BSE CD historical formats are evidenced; current NCL CD/CO and ICCL CM/FO/CO collection-report upload/response names and amendments remain incomplete. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Obtain latest client/peak-margin upload, success, partial rejection, full rejection and penalty specifications by segment. |
| [unknown] Client funds/securities/OI reporting | [unknown] Current exchange-wide funds/securities balances, allocation, OI/large-client returns, submission deadlines and rejection names remain incomplete. Old weekly/quarterly NSE schemas are not asserted as current rules. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Obtain the compliance return inventory from each exchange and reconcile to its live reporting portal. |
| [unknown] UCC acceptance and rollout | [unknown] Universal NSE create/modify response basename, latest BSE UCC batch contract, MCX v1.1 effective rollout, current rejection schemas and depository mapping requests are not fully verified. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Validate current specifications, activation status and client-level rejection handling before go-live. |
| [unknown] Settlement residuals | [unknown] Current NCL derivative cash-settlement standalone names, ICCL EPI/DPO exceptions, all bank instruction/acknowledgement files, settlement calendars for every segment and auction supplement files remain incomplete. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Obtain current settlement report catalogue from the appointed CM and clearing banks; retain securities and cash legs separately. |
| [unknown] Collateral residuals | [unknown] Current ICCL/MCXCCL haircut filenames, all pledge/repledge confirmation outputs, release/transfer rejection variants, bank guarantee/FD expiry reports and client cash/non-cash subreports are not fully established. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Reconcile collateral system, depository chain and CC valuation using live source manifests. |
| [unknown] Depository implementation | [unknown] Published v3.0.1.2 is not proof every DP is live on that version. Exact CDSL production transport, Easiest bulk formats, NSDL SPEED-e/SPICE/FAST/STeADY names, eDIS API payload versions and authorization response timing remain unknown. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Confirm DP/service entitlements and current implementation circulars; distinguish DP exports from broker/CM services. |
| [unknown] Shared containers | [unknown] Pledge, CUSPA, demat, remat, transfer and DDPI workflows can be records in shared files. Separate files per event are not established. A response can describe validation rather than final settlement. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Use transaction type, state, reference IDs and later transaction/holding evidence; do not count an intermediate upload acknowledgement as completion. |
| [unknown] Corporate actions / no-delivery | [unknown] NCL corporate-action and BSE master no-delivery fields are evidenced; standalone adjustment/no-delivery filenames for every venue and current DP corporate-action result/reversal supplements remain unverified. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Combine exchange notices, contract masters and depository movement reconciliation; verify effective dates per event. |
| [unknown] Commodity delivery ecosystem | [unknown] Complete ComRIS/warehouse/repository receipt, movement order, invoice, delivery-default/close-out and acknowledgement filenames are not established. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Obtain current commodity-specific delivery interface specifications; do not apply securities depository filenames to warehouse receipts. |
| [unknown] Times, deadlines, rates and proposals | [unknown] No unverified clock, penalty rate, percentage, circular number, form code or portal is supplied to fill a gap. Exact timings are dated, source-specific targets. Proposal and archive publication do not prove effective implementation. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Use latest effective primary notifications and member evidence; revalidate at operational onboarding. |
| [unknown] DBF/XML/fixed-width versus extension | [unknown] CSV is verified where specified; SPAN/portfolio XML and depository CSV are evidenced. Unknown TXT/DAT/LIS, legacy fixed-width or DBF details remain unknown. An extension alone does not establish encoding or delimiter. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Keep parser selection dependent on the actual schema and artifact, never a guessed extension mapping. |
| [unknown] Downstream consequences | [unknown] Consumer and breakage columns are explicitly analyst inferences; severity depends on broker architecture, redundancies and appointed CM/DP. They do not establish automatic regulatory penalties. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Turn these dependencies into locally owned ingestion/reconciliation controls. |
| [unknown] Durable knowledge-base write | [unknown] The requested research file is in the writable repository. The Obsidian vault was read, but this session has no write access to that vault. | [uncertain] Recommended verification: Copy or link this register into the broking-kyc research workspace when vault write access is available. |
